Here's a Python solution for the task:

```python
import json
import re

def extract_soil_data(text):
    data = []
    locations = text.split(';')
    for location in locations:
        if location.strip():
            params = location.split(', ')
            entry = {
                "X": None,
                "Y": None,
                "Longitude": None,
                "Latitude": None,
                "Altitude": None,
                "UD": None,
                "LD": None,
                "Average profondeur": None,
                "USDA Soil Taxonomy": None,
                "pH": None,
                "CE": None,
                "CEC": None,
                "A%": None,
                "L%": None,
                "S%": None,
                "ALR(L)": None,
                "ALR(S)": None,
                "OM": None
            }
            for param in params:
                if ':' in param:
                    key, value = param.split(': ')
                    if key == 'Latitude':
                        entry['Latitude'] = float(value)
                    elif key == 'Longitude':
                        entry['Longitude'] = float(value)
                    elif key == 'Altitude':
                        entry['Altitude'] = float(re.sub(r'[^\d\.]', '', value))
                    elif key == 'Depth':
                        depths = value.split('-')
                        entry['UD'] = float(depths[0].replace('cm', ''))
                        entry['LD'] = float(depths[1].replace('cm', ''))
                        entry['Average profondeur'] = (entry['UD'] + entry['LD']) / 2
                    elif key == 'USDA Soil Taxonomy':
                        entry[key] = value
                    else:
                        try:
                            entry[key] = float(value)
                        except ValueError:
                            entry[key] = value
            data.append(entry)
    return data

text = "Latitude: 45.764043, Longitude: 4.835659, Altitude: 200m, Depth: 0-10cm, pH: 6.5, CE: 0.5, CEC: 10, A%: 20, L%: 40, S%: 40, ALR(L): 1.2, ALR(S): 1.5, OM: 2.1, USDA Soil Taxonomy: Alfisol; Latitude: 45.764043, Longitude: 4.835659, Altitude: 200m, Depth: 10-20cm, pH: 6.8, CE: 0.6, CEC: 11, A%: 21, L%: 41, S%: 38, ALR(L): 1.3, ALR(S): 1.6, OM: 2.2, USDA Soil Taxonomy: Alfisol; Latitude: 48.856613, Longitude: 2.352222, Altitude: 100m, Depth: 0-10cm, pH: 7.0, CE: 0.7, CEC: 12, A%: 22, L%: 42, S%: 36, ALR(L): 1.4, ALR(S): 1.7, OM: 2.3, USDA Soil Taxonomy: Entisol"

print(json.dumps(extract_soil_data(text)))
```
